# Skills · E · part 12

- **eda-framework**: Produces a structured exploratory data analysis report covering row counts, column inventory, missing values, distributions, and frequency counts. Translates raw data into a complete EDA summary with specific findings, not generic descriptions. Use when the user has a dataset and wants to understand its structure, quality, and key characteristics before deeper analysis. Do NOT use for hypothesis testing (use hypothesis-testing), data cleaning operations (use data-cleaning-protocol), or building predictive models (use regression-guide). ([FerroxLabs/wayland](https://github.com/FerroxLabs/wayland/tree/HEAD/src/process/resources/skills-library/bodies/skills/data-analysis/eda-framework) · ★618 · 3 copies)

- **edges-linkedin-accept-invitation**: Accept a single LinkedIn connection invitation via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to accept a pending connection request. - **edges-linkedin-accept-invitations**: Accept multiple LinkedIn connection invitations in batch via the Edges API. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-company-employees-insights) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-connections**: Extract the full connections list from a LinkedIn identity's network via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-contact**: Extract contact information (email, phone, address) from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-contact) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-conversations**: Extract LinkedIn messaging conversations via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to list their LinkedIn message threads, check for replies in outreach, detect who replied by checking last_message sender, or audit messaging activity. Also use for sync-before-send — always extract conversations immediately before sending a message. Requires a connected identity (direct mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-conversations) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-event**: Extract LinkedIn event details via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user wants to get event information, see event description, organizer, date, or attendee count from a LinkedIn event URL. Cookieless (managed mode). - **edges-linkedin-extract-event-attendees**: Extract attendees registered for a LinkedIn event via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to get a list of people attending a LinkedIn event, build an attendee list, or prospect from event registrations. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-event-attendees) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-followers**: Extract the list of people following a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-followers) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-group-members**: Extract members of a LinkedIn group via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to get a list of people in a LinkedIn group, prospect from a group, or analyze group membership. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-group-members) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-job**: Extract details of a LinkedIn job posting via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-licenses-certifications**: Extract licenses and certifications from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-messages**: Extract messages from a LinkedIn conversation thread via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-page-followers**: Extract followers of a LinkedIn company page via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to get a list of people following a company page, audit company page followers, or prospect from a company's audience. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-page-followers) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people**: Extract full LinkedIn profile data via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to get a LinkedIn profile, enrich a person, extract profile data from a LinkedIn URL, look up someone on LinkedIn, get professional information about a person, or retrieve a profile's headline, experience, education, or skills. This is the primary profile extraction action — use extract-people-experiences for complete job history beyond the last 5, and extract-contact for visible email/phone on the profile. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-comment-activity**: Extract posts that a specific person COMMENTED ON via the Edges API. This returns posts where the person left a comment — their commenting activity, not comments on their posts. To get people who commented on a specific post, use edges-linkedin-extract-post-commenters instead. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-comment-activity) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-educations**: Extract education history from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user wants to get someone's schools, degrees, fields of study, or education background. Cookieless (managed mode). Provides more detail than the education data returned by extract-people with sections: true. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-educations) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-experiences**: Extract complete work experience history from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user needs full job history, wants all positions beyond the last 5 returned by extract-people, or needs to analyze someone's complete career trajectory. Cookieless (managed mode). Note: linkedin-extract-people only returns the last 5 experiences — use this skill for complete history paginated in chunks of 20. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-experiences) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-post-activity**: Extract posts PUBLISHED BY a specific person on LinkedIn via the Edges API. This skill returns posts that the person authored — their own content feed. Do NOT use this for finding posts that mention someone — use edges-linkedin-search-content for that instead. Use when the user wants to see what someone has been posting, analyze their content strategy, or get their recent LinkedIn posts. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-post-activity) · ★2)

Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-reaction-activity) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-people-skills**: Extract the full skills list with endorsement counts from a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. Note: linkedin-extract-people with skills: true only returns 1-2 main skills — use this skill for the complete list. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-people-skills) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-post**: Extract LinkedIn post details including content, engagement metrics, and author data via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-post-commenters**: Extract people who commented on a LinkedIn post via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-post-likers**: Extract people who liked a LinkedIn post via the Edges API. For full list without manual pagination, use async mode. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-extract-post-likers) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-extract-post-reposters**: Extract people who reposted a LinkedIn post via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-profile-viewers**: Extract people who viewed your LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-received-invitations**: Extract pending inbound LinkedIn connection invitations via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-school-alumnis**: Extract alumni from a LinkedIn school page via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-sent-invitations**: Extract pending outbound LinkedIn connection invitations via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-extract-similar-companies**: Extract companies similar to a given company on LinkedIn via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-find-company-url**: Find a company's LinkedIn page URL from their domain name or company name via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user has a domain (e.g. stripe.com) or company name and needs the verified LinkedIn URL. Always use this to resolve company URLs — never guess LinkedIn slugs. - **edges-linkedin-find-profile-url**: Find a LinkedIn profile URL by name via the Edges API. AI-powered lookup — simpler than search-people but less precise. No LinkedIn identity required. For more control over search results, use linkedin-search-people. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-find-profile-url) · ★2)

- **edges-linkedin-inmail-profile**: Send an InMail to a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-invite-event**: Invite someone to a LinkedIn event via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to send event invitations on LinkedIn. - **edges-linkedin-like-post**: Like a LinkedIn post via the Edges API. This skill should be used when the user wants to like a post on LinkedIn, react to content, or engage with someone's post as part of an outreach warmup. Requires a connected identity (direct mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-like-post) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-message-profile**: Send a direct message to a LinkedIn connection via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to message someone on LinkedIn, send a DM, follow up with a connection, or send an outreach message. Target must be a 1st-degree connection — NOT_CONNECTED error if not. Always extract-conversations first (sync-before-send). Requires a connected identity (direct mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-message-profile) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-search-companies**: Search for companies on LinkedIn via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to search LinkedIn for companies by keyword, industry, size, or location, find companies matching specific criteria, or discover companies in a particular sector. For SN company search with more filters, use salesnavigator-search-companies. For resolving a company name or domain to a LinkedIn URL, use find-company-url. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-search-companies) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-search-company-employees**: Search employees of a specific company on standard LinkedIn via the Edges API. IMPORTANT: Prefer edges-salesnavigator-search-company-employees instead — it has richer data (tenure, recently hired/promoted), more filters, and works cookieless. - **edges-linkedin-search-content**: Search LinkedIn posts and articles by keyword via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to find posts about a topic, search LinkedIn content, find posts mentioning a person or company, monitor company mentions, or discover content by keyword. Also use for finding posts FROM a company page (fromOrganization) or posts MENTIONING a company (mentionsOrganization). This is the right skill for "posts mentioning [someone]" — NOT extract-people-post-activity which returns posts published BY a person. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-search-content) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-search-events**: Search for events on LinkedIn via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-search-groups**: Search for groups on LinkedIn via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-search-jobs**: Search for job postings on LinkedIn via the Edges API. - **edges-linkedin-search-people**: Search for people on LinkedIn via the Edges API. For higher-precision searches with more filters, use salesnavigator-search-people instead. For AI-powered name-to-URL lookup, use find-profile-url. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-search-people) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-search-schools**: Search for schools on LinkedIn via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to find universities, educational institutions, or schools on LinkedIn by keyword. Cookieless (managed mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-search-schools) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-visit-company**: Visit a company page on LinkedIn to leave a view trace via the Edges API. This does NOT extract company data — it only registers a page view. To get company information, use edges-linkedin-extract-company instead. Use this as part of an outreach warm-up or monitoring workflow. Requires a connected identity (direct mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-visit-company) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-visit-profile**: Visit a LinkedIn profile via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to visit someone's profile on LinkedIn, appear in their "who viewed your profile" section, warm up a lead before connecting, or trigger profile view notifications as the first step in an outreach sequence. Requires a connected LinkedIn identity (direct mode). Usually the first step before connecting. ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-visit-profile) · ★2)
- **edges-linkedin-withdraw-invitation**: Withdraw a single pending LinkedIn connection invitation via the Edges API. Use this skill when the user wants to cancel a connection request they sent. Requires a connected identity (direct mode). ([edgesrun/linkedin-skills](https://github.com/edgesrun/linkedin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/linkedin-skills/skills/edges-linkedin-withdraw-invitation) · ★2)
